# Claim matrix · `reg:registers:claim-matrix`

Generated by the corpus linter's regeneration mode, and maintained only by
regeneration: this file is compared byte for byte on every run, and a hand
edit is a finding rather than a change.

Owner pkg.api, profile rust-test: one row per claim, ordered bytewise by claim
label. Every occurrence is a citation; the tests of a row are the test
that mints the claim and every test that cites it.

| Claim                                                                                | Statement                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Tests                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| (`claim:auth:a-breached-new-password-refuses-the-change`)                            | A new password the breach corpus knows refuses as a weak password on that field.                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:change-password-to-a-breached-password-is-weak-password`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| (`claim:auth:a-breached-password-is-refused-with-its-reason`)                        | A password the breach corpus knows is refused, and the refusal says the corpus is why.                                                                                                                                        | (`test:unit:breached-passwords-are-rejected-with-the-reason`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:auth:a-cancel-kills-both-secrets`)                                           | A cancel kills both secrets and leaves the address where it was.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:cancel-kills-both-secrets`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:auth:a-canceled-changes-link-names-the-cancel`)                              | A canceled change's link answers that the change was canceled and moves nothing.                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-canceled-changes-link-is-email-change-canceled-and-moves-nothing`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:auth:a-carve-out-collision-is-email-in-use`)                                 | The carve-out link to an address taken meanwhile answers in use and moves nothing.                                                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:the-carve-out-link-to-a-taken-address-is-email-in-use`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| (`claim:auth:a-change-past-its-window-is-expired`)                                   | A change past its window answers that it expired, by code and by link, and reads as no longer pending.                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:confirming-after-the-window-is-email-change-expired`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| (`claim:auth:a-code-without-a-change-is-a-token-error`)                              | A confirmation code with no pending change behind it is a token error.                                                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:a-garbage-code-with-no-pending-change-is-a-token-error`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| (`claim:auth:a-collision-is-reported-and-the-change-survives-it`)                    | An address taken between the two proofs reports as in use rather than as a transport fault, and the change row stays live so a retry inside the window still says why and still applies once the address frees.               | (`test:integration:a-collision-surfaces-email-in-use-and-retries-stay-truthful`) (`test:integration:a-proven-email-change-colliding-with-a-registered-address-reports-in-use`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:auth:a-device-label-stops-at-a-hundred-characters`)                          | A device label is capped at a hundred Unicode scalar values, trimmed, with blank folding to absent.                                                                                                                           | (`test:unit:a-device-label-is-capped-at-a-hundred-characters-and-blank-folds`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:auth:a-failed-login-neither-leaks-nor-spends-the-mark`)                      | A failed login neither leaks the pending reuse mark nor spends it, so the notice still waits for a real one.                                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:a-refusal-never-carries-or-clears-the-notice`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (`claim:auth:a-grace-replay-is-idempotent`)                                          | An immediate replay inside the grace window is handed the same successor rather than an error or a fork, and no detection fires.                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-grace-window-replay-returns-the-same-successor`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| (`claim:auth:a-handle-change-answers-to-the-one-namespace`)                          | A handle change answers to the one namespace every actor kind shares.                                                                                                                                                         | (`test:integration:handle-changes-respect-the-one-namespace`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:auth:a-handle-folds-to-one-spelling`)                                        | A handle is checked against its own grammar and folded to one canonical spelling.                                                                                                                                             | (`test:unit:handles-fold-and-validate`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:auth:a-held-handle-refuses-the-change`)                                      | A handle another account holds refuses the change as taken on that field.                                                                                                                                                     | (`test:integration:change-handle-to-a-held-handle-is-handle-taken`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| (`claim:chronicle:the-cited-side-reads-its-citations`)                               | A cited node reads what points at it off the terminal leg, newest first and carrying the pair each citing author signed, where its own outbound citation rides the anchored read instead.                                     | (`test:integration:the-cited-side-reads-its-citations`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:chronicle:the-filters-narrow-the-anchored-page`)                             | Every declared filter narrows the anchored page, and a sign filter agrees with the parameters the page's own edges carry.                                                                                                     | (`test:integration:the-filters-narrow-the-anchored-page`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:chronicle:the-pair-anchors-on-the-leg-ends`)                                 | Outgoing anchors on the legs a node is the source of and incoming on the legs it is the target of, so a node that authors nothing has an empty outgoing chronicle.                                                            | (`test:integration:the-pair-anchors-on-the-ends-of-a-leg`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| (`claim:compression:identity-without-accept-encoding`)                               | The feed answer is identity-encoded without Accept-Encoding.                                                                                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:the-feed-answer-is-identity-without-accept-encoding`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| (`claim:compression:no-mutation-answer-compresses`)                                  | A mutation's answer is never compressed, so session material never meets the compressor.                                                                                                                                      | (`test:integration:a-session-minting-answer-is-never-compressed`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (`claim:compression:no-query-reaches-session-material`)                              | No type a query can reach carries a token or a secret.                                                                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:no-query-reaches-session-material`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| (`claim:compression:the-feed-answer-compresses`)                                     | The feed answer is gzip-compressed when the client accepts gzip, decoding to the identical JSON.                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:the-feed-answer-is-compressed-when-the-client-accepts-gzip`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:constants:every-terminal-refusal-is-a-published-code`)                       | Every exported terminal refusal is a code the schema actually publishes.                                                                                                                                                      | (`test:integration:the-terminal-refusals-are-published-error-codes`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:constants:the-committed-constants-match-what-the-crate-derives`)             | The committed client constants match what this crate derives, so drift fails the build rather than reaching a client.                                                                                                         | (`test:integration:exported-constants-match-the-committed-file`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| (`claim:constants:the-exported-caps-are-the-enforced-caps`)                          | The exported media caps are the caps the server itself enforces, not a second statement of them.                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:the-exported-caps-are-the-ones-the-server-enforces`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:content:a-body-is-words-or-media`)                                           | A post's body is words or media and never both or neither, refused from both sides on a create and on an edit alike.                                                                                                          | (`test:integration:a-post-body-is-words-or-media-and-never-both-or-neither`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| (`claim:content:a-comment-body-stops-at-two-thousand-characters`)                    | A comment's body is capped at two thousand Unicode scalar values on a create and on an edit alike.                                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-comment-body-is-capped-at-two-thousand-characters`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| (`claim:content:a-comment-carries-a-video-at-half-the-budget`)                       | A comment carries one video at half a post's byte budget, the cap being applied where the parent is finally known.                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-comments-video-is-capped-at-half-a-posts`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:a-comment-carries-the-same-mark`)                                    | A comment seals through the same seal a post does, so it carries the same mark and veils the same way.                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:a-self-marked-comment-veils-its-body`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:content:a-comments-gallery-is-bounded-lower`)                                | A comment's gallery is bounded lower than a post's.                                                                                                                                                                           | (`test:integration:a-comment-gallery-stops-at-four`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:content:a-comments-media-sits-beside-its-words`)                             | A comment's media supports its words rather than replacing them: the smaller gallery, and no cover.                                                                                                                           | (`test:integration:a-comment-carries-media-beside-its-words`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:content:a-description-stops-at-five-hundred-characters`)                     | A post description is capped at five hundred Unicode scalar values on a create and on an edit alike, with a blank description folding to absent.                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-post-description-is-capped-at-five-hundred-characters`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| (`claim:content:a-gallery-is-rebuildable-from-its-record`)                           | A gallery is on the version row and its manifest on the record, the two saying the same thing in the same order, so a gallery is rebuildable from the record alone.                                                           | (`test:integration:a-media-post-lands-with-its-manifest-and-its-junction-rows`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:a-gallery-refuses-whole-and-names-its-offender`)                     | Every gallery fault refuses the gallery whole and names the entry that caused it, someone else's picture included.                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-gallery-is-refused-whole-and-names-the-offending-entry`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:content:a-landed-post-leaves-all-three-traces`)                              | A landed post leaves all three traces at once: the display row bound to its node in landing order, the exact envelope bytes in carriage, and the record in the chronicle.                                                     | (`test:integration:a-post-lands-with-carriage-display-row-and-envelope-binding`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| (`claim:content:a-licence-off-the-square-is-refused`)                                | A licence a degree off the square or off the grid entirely is refused at construction.                                                                                                                                        | (`test:unit:checked-refuses-degrees-off-the-square-or-off-the-grid`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:content:a-pick-only-edit-is-a-version`)                                      | A post edit whose only change is the stance pick signs and lands as a new version carrying that pick, the content as it stood.                                                                                                | (`test:integration:a-pick-only-post-edit-lands-as-a-version-carrying-the-pick`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:a-post-body-stops-at-five-thousand-characters`)                      | A post's words-body is capped at five thousand Unicode scalar values on a create and on an edit alike.                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:a-post-body-is-capped-at-five-thousand-characters`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| (`claim:content:a-post-round-trips-to-an-anonymous-reader`)                          | A post composed and signed on a device reads back anonymously through every read shape, the shared graph needing no session, and another member's comment serves under it in the thread.                                      | (`test:integration:post-from-the-phone-read-it-back`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| (`claim:content:a-poster-is-a-placement-fact-an-edit-can-swap`)                      | A poster is authored on the placement, witnessed in the record, and swapped by an edit without the clip moving.                                                                                                               | (`test:integration:an-edit-swaps-the-poster-without-touching-the-clip`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:content:a-poster-must-be-the-uploaders-own-still`)                           | A poster must be a still this account uploaded and still holds, only a video placement may name one, and a clip may go without.                                                                                               | (`test:integration:a-poster-outside-the-rules-is-refused`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| (`claim:content:a-posts-video-cap-is-enforced-too`)                                  | A video larger than a post's own cap is refused at the attachment.                                                                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-video-past-a-posts-cap-is-refused`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| (`claim:content:a-reason-needs-its-switch`)                                          | A blank reason is no reason and a reason without the switch is refused, so the author never signs a warning nobody is shown.                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:a-self-mark-reconciles-its-switch-and-its-reason`) (`test:integration:a-sensitive-reason-without-the-mark-is-refused`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:content:a-self-mark-veils-the-body-not-the-title`)                           | A self-marked post veils its whole body as one region while the title stays readable, so choosing to look is an informed choice.                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-self-marked-post-veils-its-body-and-keeps-its-title`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| (`claim:content:a-sensitive-reason-stops-at-a-hundred-forty-characters`)             | A self-mark's reason is capped at a hundred forty Unicode scalar values.                                                                                                                                                      | (`test:integration:a-sensitive-reason-is-capped-at-a-hundred-forty-characters`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:a-still-carries-equal-digests`)                                      | A still carries its rendition and upload digests both, equal because it was stored as it arrived.                                                                                                                             | (`test:integration:a-still-carries-equal-rendition-and-upload-digests`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:content:a-superseded-version-keeps-its-gallery`)                             | A superseded version keeps the gallery it carried, so a picture an edit dropped is still referenced and survives the orphan sweep.                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-superseded-version-keeps-its-own-gallery`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:a-tier-renders-canonically`)                                         | Each published licence tier renders to its own canonical string.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:unit:canonical-renders-the-published-tiers`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| (`claim:content:a-title-stops-at-a-hundred-characters`)                              | A post title is capped at a hundred Unicode scalar values on a create and on an edit alike, with a blank title folding to absent.                                                                                             | (`test:integration:a-post-title-is-capped-at-a-hundred-characters`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| (`claim:content:a-video-is-the-whole-body`)                                          | A body is pictures or one video: a video sharing a gallery with another asset is refused, and a video alone lands.                                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-video-may-not-share-a-body-with-pictures`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| (`claim:content:a-zero-stance-creation-is-legal`)                                    | Creating at the zero stance is legal: a post at attachment zero and a comment at the zero pair both prepare and land.                                                                                                         | (`test:integration:a-creation-at-the-zero-stance-prepares-and-lands`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| (`claim:content:an-edit-is-a-whole-snapshot-chained-behind-the-last`)                | An edit's payload is a whole snapshot and not a patch, an absent field storing null, and each edit chains behind the one before it.                                                                                           | (`test:integration:a-post-edit-replaces-the-snapshot-and-appends-a-version`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

| (`claim:content:an-expired-edit-rolls-back-whole`)                                   | An expired edit rolls its words and its gallery back together, so a reader never meets the old words under the new pictures.                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:an-expired-edit-rolls-back-the-words-and-the-gallery-together`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| (`claim:content:an-unpublished-licence-string-is-refused`)                           | A licence string this instance never published is refused rather than guessed at.                                                                                                                                             | (`test:unit:parse-refuses-what-cogra-never-published`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| (`claim:content:an-unsigned-edit-is-superseded`)                                     | An unsigned edit is superseded by the next one and can no longer be signed, so the next edit lands without waiting for GC.                                                                                                    | (`test:integration:an-unsigned-edit-is-superseded-by-the-next`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:content:comments-thread-under-comments-too`)                                 | A comment is minted on its own terminal leg, so comments thread under comments as readily as under posts, and commenting on nothing refuses.                                                                                  | (`test:integration:comments-thread-and-edit-on-posts-and-comments`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| (`claim:content:published-licence-strings-stay-readable`)                            | Every licence string the retired ladder ever published still parses, so no landed record becomes unreadable.                                                                                                                  | (`test:unit:every-string-the-retired-ladder-published-still-parses`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| (`claim:content:the-licence-grid-round-trips`)                                       | Every point of the licence grid parses back to the value its canonical rendering came from.                                                                                                                                   | (`test:unit:canonical-round-trips-across-the-whole-grid`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:content:the-licence-square-is-admitted-whole`)                               | The checked constructor admits every licence the square defines.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:unit:checked-accepts-the-whole-square`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:content:the-listing-pages-by-keyset-both-ways`)                              | The listing is newest-first by landing order, and its keyset cursor continues exactly where a page ended, forward and backward alike.                                                                                         | (`test:integration:the-listing-pages-by-keyset-in-landing-order`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (`claim:content:the-self-mark-is-witnessed`)                                         | The self-mark is witnessed: it rides the envelope the device signs, and the display row is that payload's projection.                                                                                                         | (`test:integration:a-self-mark-rides-the-signed-payload-into-the-display-row`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:content:the-two-refusal-tiers-stay-apart`)                                   | The two refusal tiers stay apart: an unresolvable target is a user error pinned to its field, while an out-of-range parameter never reaches the resolver at all.                                                              | (`test:integration:a-refused-prepare-reports-user-errors`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| (`claim:content:writes-are-member-gated-and-reads-are-not`)                          | Writing content needs a member session where reading needs none.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:content-writes-need-a-member-session`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:custody:a-discarded-lock-is-not-found`)                                      | A discarded device lock is NOT_FOUND at release.                                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-discarded-lock-is-not-found`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| (`claim:custody:an-issue-returns-a-fresh-32-byte-secret`)                            | Issuing a device lock hands the device a fresh 32-byte secret under its own id, and the server keeps exactly that secret.                                                                                                     | (`test:integration:issuing-a-device-lock-returns-a-fresh-32-byte-secret`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:custody:another-accounts-lock-is-not-found`)                                 | Another account's device lock is NOT_FOUND, even behind that caller's own right password.                                                                                                                                     | (`test:integration:another-accounts-lock-is-not-found`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:custody:discard-is-idempotent-and-silent`)                                   | Discarding a device lock is idempotent and answers an unknown id exactly like a known one.                                                                                                                                    | (`test:integration:discarding-a-lock-is-idempotent-and-silent-for-unknown-ids`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| (`claim:custody:session-revocations-never-touch-locks`)                              | No session revocation of any kind touches a device lock.                                                                                                                                                                      | (`test:integration:session-revocations-never-touch-device-locks`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (`claim:custody:the-current-password-releases-the-secret`)                           | A device lock releases its own secret behind the account's current password.                                                                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:releasing-a-lock-with-the-current-password-returns-its-secret`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| (`claim:custody:the-live-cap-holds-under-concurrent-issues`)                         | Concurrent issues never carry an account past its live-lock cap.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:the-live-lock-cap-holds-under-concurrent-issues`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:custody:the-old-password-cannot-release-after-a-change`)                     | After a password change on another device the old password cannot release a device lock and the new one can.                                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:the-old-password-cannot-release-after-a-change-elsewhere`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| (`claim:keys:a-backup-blob-round-trips-and-replacement-overwrites`)                  | A signed backup blob round-trips, and a replacement overwrites the one row the account keeps rather than accumulating beside it.                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-signed-upload-roundtrips-and-replacement-overwrites`) (`test:integration:key-backup-replacement-overwrites-the-one-row`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:keys:a-challenge-is-single-use`)                                             | A challenge is spent by the upload it authorizes, so replaying the very same bytes and signature is refused.                                                                                                                  | (`test:integration:a-challenge-is-single-use`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:keys:a-challenge-needs-a-session`)                                           | Taking a challenge needs a session.                                                                                                                                                                                           | (`test:integration:the-challenge-needs-a-session`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| (`claim:keys:a-refusal-does-not-spend-the-challenge`)                                | A wrong-key signature is refused without spending the challenge, so a wrong attempt costs the rightful holder no fresh round trip.                                                                                            | (`test:integration:a-bad-signature-does-not-burn-the-challenge`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| (`claim:keys:a-session-alone-cannot-overwrite-the-blob`)                             | A live session is not enough to overwrite the blob: without the actor key nothing signed verifies, so a stolen login cannot take the account's recovery path.                                                                 | (`test:integration:a-session-without-the-actor-key-cannot-overwrite-the-blob`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:keys:a-signature-authorizes-only-its-own-bytes`)                             | A signature authorizes the bytes it was made over and no others.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:a-signature-for-other-bytes-does-not-authorize-this-blob`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| (`claim:keys:an-expired-challenge-refuses`)                                          | A challenge past its lifetime refuses.                                                                                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:an-expired-challenge-refuses`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| (`claim:keys:an-uninvited-challenge-refuses`)                                        | A challenge this server never issued refuses, so one cannot simply be invented.                                                                                                                                               | (`test:integration:a-challenge-never-issued-refuses`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| (`claim:keys:at-most-one-challenge-stands`)                                          | Issuing a challenge discards the previous one, so at most one ever stands per account.                                                                                                                                        | (`test:integration:issuing-again-discards-the-previous-challenge`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| (`claim:keys:malformed-encoding-names-its-field`)                                    | Malformed encoding is refused against the field that carried it.                                                                                                                                                              | (`test:integration:malformed-base64-refuses-on-the-offending-field`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:keys:no-attached-key-is-no-backup`)                                          | An account with no attached key can neither take a challenge nor upload, there being nothing to verify a signature against.                                                                                                   | (`test:integration:an-account-with-no-attached-key-cannot-challenge-or-upload`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| (`claim:keys:only-a-replacement-mails-the-notice`)                                   | The first upload destroys no recovery path and stays silent; every later one replaces one and mails the notice.                                                                                                               | (`test:integration:only-replacement-mails-the-notice`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| (`claim:keys:the-backup-date-is-viewer-only`)                                        | The backup's date resolves only for the account's own viewer.                                                                                                                                                                 | (`test:integration:key-backup-created-at-is-viewer-only`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:keys:the-backup-date-reads-the-newest-upload`)                               | The backup's date is the newest upload's, restamped by every replacement.                                                                                                                                                     | (`test:integration:key-backup-created-at-reads-the-newest-backup`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| (`claim:keys:the-cap-is-on-the-decoded-blob`)                                        | The size cap is on the decoded blob, and one byte over refuses while leaving the store untouched.                                                                                                                             | (`test:integration:a-blob-over-the-cap-refuses-and-stores-nothing`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| (`claim:mail:the-sink-is-created-and-appended-in-order`)                             | The configured sink is created on the way and every message appends to it in send order under a dated header.                                                                                                                 | (`test:unit:appends-each-message-to-the-configured-file`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| (`claim:onboarding:the-attach-guards-hold-until-funding-binds`)                      | The ceremony's outputs must cohere before anything is stored, re-attaching the account's own current key is the crash-healing repair rather than a conflict, and the funding approval triggers binds the address for good.    | (`test:integration:the-attach-guards-hold`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| (`claim:profile:an-empty-update-is-refused`)                                         | An update carrying no change is refused as the empty gesture it is.                                                                                                                                                           | (`test:integration:a-picture-alone-is-enough-to-be-an-update`) (`test:integration:empty-update-refuses-with-a-user-error`) (`test:integration:refuses-an-empty-update`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| (`claim:profile:an-expired-update-falls-back-to-the-previous-version`)               | An expired profile update leaves no row behind, and the previous version renders again.                                                                                                                                       | (`test:integration:an-expired-update-falls-back-to-the-previous-version`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:profile:an-unsigned-update-is-superseded`)                                   | An unsigned prepared update is superseded by the next prepare and can no longer be signed, so the next update lands without waiting for GC.                                                                                   | (`test:integration:an-unsigned-update-is-superseded-by-the-next`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| (`claim:profile:an-update-lands-a-merged-version-and-its-envelope`)                  | A landed update appends a merged version row and puts its envelope into permanent carriage, the anchor's own empty payload never reaching carriage at all.                                                                    | (`test:integration:profile-update-lands-a-merged-version-row`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:profile:an-update-needs-its-anchor`)                                         | An update needs the anchoring Registration to chain behind.                                                                                                                                                                   | (`test:integration:refuses-an-update-without-the-anchoring-registration`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:profile:the-actor-lookup-takes-exactly-one-argument`)                        | The actor lookup resolves to the concrete type behind the interface and takes exactly one argument, both and neither being transport faults.                                                                                  | (`test:integration:actor-resolves-and-argument-rule-holds`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| (`claim:profile:the-avatar-slot-carries-both-digests`)                               | The avatar slot carries both the rendition digest and the upload digest.                                                                                                                                                      | (`test:integration:the-avatar-slot-carries-both-digests`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| (`claim:profile:the-text-fields-answer-to-their-own-caps`)                           | A profile's display name, bio, and website URL are each capped at their own length, in Unicode scalar values.                                                                                                                 | (`test:integration:the-text-fields-are-capped-at-their-own-lengths`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:profile:updates-are-serialized-per-profile`)                                 | One update at a time per profile: a second prepare is refused while the first is signed and unlanded, and landing the first frees the chain.                                                                                  | (`test:integration:refuses-a-second-in-flight-update`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| (`claim:profile:updates-chain-and-merge-per-field`)                                  | A second update asserts the current chain head as its parent and merges per field, so clearing one field leaves the rest as the first update set them.                                                                        | (`test:integration:second-update-chains-behind-the-head-and-merges`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:ratelimit:a-batch-is-budgeted-whole`)                                        | A creation batch the claim budget cannot carry is refused entire, staging nothing and spending nothing of any window.                                                                                                         | (`test:integration:a-creation-batch-over-the-claim-budget-is-refused-whole-and-stages-nothing`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
