| (`claim:ratelimit:both-upload-paths-share-one-budget`)                               | Whole and resumable uploads spend one per-account budget, and past it either path is refused at the transport.                                                                                                                | (`test:integration:uploads-trip-the-account-budget`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| (`claim:ratelimit:comments-spend-their-own-budget`)                                  | Comments spend a window of their own, and the one past it is refused as a write-rule failure.                                                                                                                                 | (`test:integration:comment-budget-trips-as-write-rule-failed`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| (`claim:ratelimit:edits-share-one-budget`)                                           | Every edit, whatever it edits, draws on one shared edit budget.                                                                                                                                                               | (`test:integration:edit-budget-trips-as-write-rule-failed`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

| (`claim:ratelimit:the-backoff-is-no-existence-oracle`)                               | The backoff must not become an account-existence oracle, so an email with no account behind it blocks after the same failures with the same refusal.                                                                          | (`test:integration:login-backoff-arms-for-unknown-accounts-identically`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| (`claim:references:parameters-are-written-as-sent`)                                  | The draft carries no optional parameter, so a citation is planned with exactly the pair the user signed for and nothing the server filled in.                                                                                 | (`test:integration:a-standalone-citation-lands-its-parameters-as-signed`) (`test:unit:reference-parameters-are-written-exactly-as-sent`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| (`claim:references:the-balance-carries-the-whole-gesture-or-none`)                   | Either the balance carries the whole gesture or none of it stages, so an author never reads one gesture and gets an arbitrary prefix of it.                                                                                   | (`test:integration:a-balance-that-cannot-carry-the-batch-refuses-all-of-it`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
